# Agents · D · part 8

1,033 entries, deadline-watcher to debugger. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **deadline-watcher**: 定时运行的诉讼/仲裁期限监控代理——跟踪开庭、举证期限、答辩期、管辖权异议、 上诉期、再审申请、申请执行、保全续封续冻、仲裁时效等带硬性法定期限的事项， 在期限错过之前触发"决策点"预警。不是状态面板；它告诉你"现在需要做什么决策、 截止到何时"。建议每周运行（开庭/举证临近 14 天内的事项改为每日）。自动定时 需要单独的集成——Claude Code 代理不会自我调度。 触发词："期限检查""有什么期限""开庭安排""上诉期到了吗""检查台账""期限预警"。 ([FAYANHUIYING/claude-for-legal-HoriZon](https://github.com/FAYANHUIYING/claude-for-legal-HoriZon/blob/HEAD/dispute-resolution/agents/deadline-watcher.md) · ★2)
- **deadline-watcher**: Scheduled agent that checks the tax deadline calendar and posts what's coming up. Runs weekly by default. Posts to the channel named in `~/.claude/plugins/config/claude-for-tax/corporate-tax/CLAUDE.md` → House style → Deadline alerts. Trigger phrases: "what tax deadlines are coming", "check tax deadlines", "deadline report", or on schedule. ([hazlijohar95/claude-tax-malaysia](https://github.com/hazlijohar95/claude-tax-malaysia/blob/HEAD/corporate-tax/agents/deadline-watcher.md) · ★1)
- **deadline-watcher**: Scheduled agent that checks the individual tax deadline calendar and posts what's coming up. Runs weekly by default. Trigger phrases: "what personal tax deadlines are coming", "check tax deadlines", "deadline report", or on schedule. ([hazlijohar95/claude-tax-malaysia](https://github.com/hazlijohar95/claude-tax-malaysia/blob/HEAD/personal-tax/agents/deadline-watcher.md) · ★1)
- **deadline-watcher**: Scheduled agent that checks the payroll-tax deadline calendar and posts what's coming up — the monthly PCB and EPF/SOCSO/EIS remittances, the annual Form E / EA dates, and any open CP21 / CP22A windows. Runs weekly by default. Posts to the channel named in `~/.claude/plugins/config/claude-for-tax/employment-tax/CLAUDE.md` → House style → Remittance / deadline alerts. Trigger phrases: "what payroll deadlines are coming", "check payroll tax deadlines", "deadline report", or on schedule. - **deadline-watcher**: Scheduled agent that sweeps the controversy portfolio for objection, appeal, and response deadlines and posts what's coming up — daily by default, because the objection window is unforgiving. Posts to the channel in `~/.claude/plugins/config/claude-for-tax/controversy-tax/CLAUDE.md` → House style → Deadline alerts. Trigger phrases: "check dispute deadlines", "deadline sweep", or on schedule. - **deadline-watcher**: Sweeps pending_receipts/ and submission folders, reports items overdue against the institution's filing-deadline rules, sorted by urgency. Read-only. - **deadline-watcher**: Reads the household's obligations register and reports what is overdue, due soon, or inside its lead time, so that a closing window is never missed. Read-only: it changes nothing and asks nothing. Use for a scheduled check (for example every morning or every Monday) or when someone asks to "check deadlines in the background" or "watch our renewals". - **deadlock-analyzer**: .NET 10 async 코드를 정적 분석해 데드락·스레드풀 기아·해제 누락을 문맥(library/app/test)별 조건부 위험으로 보고한다. deadlock-reviewer가 검증한다. ([BerryBless/WebProject](https://github.com/BerryBless/WebProject/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/deadlock-analyzer.md))
- **deadlock-log-reader**: Read the deadlock reports in a PostgreSQL server log or a MySQL error log (innodb_print_all_deadlocks output) and return the cycle class of each distinct pattern, with counts and the log line that decided it, so the log itself never enters the caller's context. Use when a log file holds many deadlock entries and someone needs to know which patterns repeat and how often before deciding on a change. It classifies and counts; it never proposes the code fix, which stays with the deadlock-analysis skill. Not for a blocking chain queueing on a live server right now (lock-queue-triage). ([greenblacked/AI](https://github.com/greenblacked/AI/blob/HEAD/plugins/databases/agents/deadlock-log-reader.md))
- **deadlock-reviewer**: Audits Neru changes for mode-handler locking regressions such as locked-context re-entry, deferred callbacks taking the lock synchronously, and lock-order inversions. Use before merging changes under internal/app/modes/**, internal/app/services/**, or anything that adds timers, goroutines, or callbacks reaching modes.Handler. - **debruijn**: The formalize lane's planner — de Bruijn, the Lean 4 engineer, on Opus.
